# Felicity Solar (fSolar) OpenAPI — Reference

Source: https://docs.apipost.net/docs/detail/533ebd61e043000 (Apipost project "openApi", branch `main`, last updated 2026-04-17T23:24:14+08:00).
Extracted from the Apipost share API (`/doc/preview`, `/doc/details`). Content is reproduced as published (generated by `tools/build-reference.js`); see [REVIEW.md](REVIEW.md) for the problems found in it.

**Environments listed in the share:** Mock Environment → https://mock.apipost.net/mock/533ea02c9443000

**Global parameters:** none · **Global auth:** noauth · **Global response codes:** none defined

## Contents

- Public Infomation
- User-related
  - `POST` Refresh Token — `/openApi/sec/refreshToken`
  - `POST` login — `/openApi/sec/login`
  - `GET` Get Current User Info — `/openApi/sec/userInfo`
- Device Data
  - `GET` Device Energy data query for year, month, day — `/openApi/data/deviceDataEnergy`
  - `GET` Plant Energy data query for year, month, day — `/openApi/data/plantDataEnergy`
  - `GET` Device Basic Data Query — `/openApi/data/deviceDataBasic/{deviceSn}`
  - `GET` Historical real-time data query (single device) — `/openApi/data/deviceDataHistory/{deviceSn}`
  - `GET` Historical real-time data query (batch device) — `/openApi/data/devicesDataHistory`
  - `GET` Event Query (alarms and faults) — `/openApi/data/deviceDataWarn/{deviceSn}`
  - The document provides an explanation of the differences in historical data for devices.
    - T-REX
    - IVGM
- Plant Interface
  - `GET` Plants list — `/openApi/plants/list`
  - `GET` plant Power Data — `/openApi/data/plantPowerData/{plantId}`
- Device Interface
  - `POST` Deleting devices in bulk — `/openApi/devices/del`
  - `GET` Device list — `/openApi/devices/list`
  - `POST` Bulk device addition — `/openApi/devices/reg`
- Remote Control 
  - `GET` Query Remote Control Setting Params — `/openApi/cmd/deviceSetting/params`
  - `POST` Remote Control Setting — `/openApi/cmd/deviceSetting`
  - Remote Control Setting Attachment (T-REX、IVGM)
  - Remote Control Setting Attachment (IVEM、IVBM、IVPM、IVCM、IVAM、IVPS、IVPA)
  - `GET` Query Remote Control Setting value — `/openApi/cmd/deviceSetting/{deviceSn}`
  - Query Remote Control Setting Attachment
  - Query Remote Control Setting Attachment (IVEM、IVBM、IVPM、IVCM、IVAM、IVPS、IVPA)

## Public Infomation

### Server URL：
All APIs need to be prefixed with server URL： https://open-api.felicitysolar.com

For example：
    API Refresh Token，URI is '/openApi/sec/refreshToken' , When calling this API, the complete URL is 'https://open-api.felicitysolar.com/openApi/sec/refreshToken'

## User-related

### Refresh Token

`POST /openApi/sec/refreshToken`  
_Status: Completed · Auth: inherit_

This endpoint allows you to refresh the authentication token by passing the refresh token obtained from the login endpoint as a parameter.

**If the refresh token expires, you need to log in again.**

error code

| code |  |
| --- | --- |
| 999 | You need to log in again. |
| 1002001 | Account not activated; please contact the administrator. |

**Body parameters (json)**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `refreshToken` | string | yes |  | Refresh Token |

**Body example**

```json
{
  "refreshToken": "Bearer_eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJpZCI6OTAyMTE1NDA0NDk4ODg5NiwiaWF0IjoxNzE4ODYzODM0fQ._dSC4TGSdeBrK7fbpHcijo23GohZcOK4SMUirToHP9811"
}
```

**Response — Success (200)**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `code` | number | yes | 200 | Response code |
| `message` | string | yes | Success | Response message body |
| `data` | object | yes |  | Returned data |
| `data.token` | string | yes | Bearer_eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJkZW1vVXNlciI6ZmFsc2UsInN1YiI6 | Authentication token |
| `data.tokenExpireTime` | string | yes | 1721456219000 | Authentication token expiration time (timestamp) |
| `data.refreshToken` | string | yes | Bearer_eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJpZCI6OTAyMTE1NDA0NDk4ODg5Niwi | Refresh token |
| `data.refTokenExpireTime` | string | yes | 1724047834012 | Refresh token expiration time |

```json

  "message": "Success",
  "data": {
    "token": "Bearer_eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJkZW1vVXNlciI6ZmFsc2UsInN1YiI6Iui-o-akkiIsImF1ZCI6Ik9UQXlNVEUxTkRBME5EazRPRGc1Tmc9PSIsIm5iZiI6MTcxODg2NDIxOSwiaXNzIjoiRkxTLVNPTEFSLVNFUlZFUiIsImlkIjo5MDIxMTU0MDQ0OTg4ODk2LCJleHAiOjE3MjE0NTYyMTksImlhdCI6MTcxODg2NDIxOX0.T_uFdGuJJm2bssgTiHTRuzpRv17q7_sgYkOraAFdXBk",
```

### login

`POST /openApi/sec/login`  
_Status: Completed · Auth: inherit_

1.First, We need to encrypt the password for transmission using the RSA algorithm.The steps are as follows：

1.1 Use the plaintext password (account password) and the public Key provided by fSolar to compute the encrypted password using the RSA algorithm.
1.2 Use the encrypted password to fill the password field in the login interface, and provide the correct username. Request the login interface to obtain the token.
1.3 When accessing other interfaces, fill in the parameters according to the interface documentation and include the required token. This will allow you to access the interfaces.

Public key
`MFwwDQYJKoZIhvcNAQEBBQADSwAwSAJBAK0GDivaRzIKeTmQnAxAYh2LChuHWDp0yHZ0zIvm+Eoi7J+rx7phqR7EtkBDO3HWqAXVkNDeeQaU32P5w1Q4FVUCAwEAAQ==`

Java encryption example
```java
import javax.crypto.Cipher;
import java.security.KeyFactory;
import java.security.PublicKey;
import java.security.spec.X509EncodedKeySpec;
import java.util.Base64;

    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
        String password = "8888888";
        //public key
        String publicKeyStr = "MFwwDQYJKoZIhvcNAQEBBQADSwAwSAJBAK0GDivaRzIKeTmQnAxAYh2LChuHWDp0yHZ0zIvm+Eoi7J+rx7phqR7EtkBDO3HWqAXVkNDeeQaU32P5w1Q4FVUCAwEAAQ==";

        PublicKey publicKey = KeyFactory.getInstance("RSA")
                .generatePublic(new X509EncodedKeySpec(Base64.getDecoder().decode(publicKeyStr)));

        // encrypt data using a public key
        Cipher encryptCipher = Cipher.getInstance("RSA");
        encryptCipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, publicKey);
        byte[] encryptedBytes = encryptCipher.doFinal(password.getBytes());
        System.out.println("encrypted data: " + Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(encryptedBytes));
    }
```
error code

| code |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1002006 | Wrong password |

**Body parameters (json)**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `password` | string | yes |  | password |
| `userName` | string | yes |  | username:ps:The account is provided by our company |

**Body example**

```json
{
  "password": "KnihfiVSe7WGt43EtmMGfkbPorIPLitSo5Ue3PkoxO1CP1GOusglHRLmvHKnNXTLIhrV1n3o2nfrBfJSKizNkg==",
  "userName": "11@qq.com"
}
```

**Response — Success (200)**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `code` | number | yes | 200 | code |
| `message` | string | yes | Success | message |
| `data` | object | yes |  | data |
| `data.token` | string | yes | Bearer_eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJkZW1vVXNlciI6ZmFsc2UsInN1YiI6 | Authentication token |
| `data.tokenExpireTime` | TimeStamp | yes | 1721456219000 | Authentication token expiration time |
| `data.refreshToken` | string | yes | Bearer_eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJpZCI6OTAyMTE1NDA0NDk4ODg5Niwi | Refresh authentication token |
| `data.refTokenExpireTime` | TimeStamp | yes | 1724047834008 | Refresh authentication token expiration time |

```json

  "code": 200,
  "message": "Success",
  "data": {
    "token": "Bearer_eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJkZW1vVXNlciI6ZmFsc2UsInN1YiI6Iui-o-akkiIsImF1ZCI6Ik9UQXlNVEUxTkRBME5EazRPRGc1Tmc9PSIsIm5iZiI6MTcxODg2NDIxOSwiaXNzIjoiRkxTLVNPTEFSLVNFUlZFUiIsImlkIjo5MDIxMTU0MDQ0OTg4ODk2LCJleHAiOjE3MjE0NTYyMTksImlhdCI6MTcxODg2NDIxOX0.T_uFdGuJJm2bssgTiHTRuzpRv17q7_sgYkOraAFdXBk",
```

## Device Data

### Device Energy data query for year, month, day

`GET /openApi/data/deviceDataEnergy`  
_Status: Completed · Auth: inherit_

This endpoint adjusts the response data based on the timeDimension parameter. Depending on whether it's set to day, month, year, or total, the data values in the response vary accordingly.Energy response unit kWh precision to two decimal places

**Headers**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Authorization` | string | yes | Bearer_eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJkZW1vVXNlciI6ZmFsc2UsInN1YiI6 | authentication token  |

**Body parameters (form-data)**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `deviceSn` | string | yes | 020505004822242582 | device Sn |
| `timeDimension` | string | yes | day | Query dimension :day month year total , when dimension  is total response all , dateStr can be empty |
| `dateStr` | string | no |  | Query time: (yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss) |
| `pageNum` | number | yes | 1 | Page number (default 1) |
| `pageSize` | number | yes | 10 | PageSize，The maximum value is 300 |

**Response — Success (200)**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `code` | number | yes | 200 | Code |
| `message` | string | yes | Success | Message |
| `data` | object | yes |  | Return Data |
| `data.records` | object | yes |  | records |
| `data.records.deviceSn` | string | yes | 020505004822242582 | Device serial number |
| `data.records.dataTime` | string | yes | 2023-04-03 | Data time |
| `data.records.timeStamp` | number | yes | 1680451200000 | Device time (timestamp) converted based on UTC+8 time zone |
| `data.records.gridInput` | Double | yes |  | Grid input Energy (kWh) |
| `data.records.feedOutput` | Double | yes |  | Grid feed Energy (kWh) |
| `data.records.generateEnergy` | Double | yes |  | Power generation |
| `data.records.inversionEnergy` | Double | yes |  | Inversion Energy |
| `data.records.batCharEnergy` | Double | yes |  | Battery charge energy(kWh) |
| `data.records.bat2ChartEnergy` | Double | yes |  | Battery2 charge energy(kWh) |
| `data.records.batDisEnergy` | Double | yes |  | Battery discharge energy(kWh) |
| `data.records.batDis2Energy` | Double | yes |  | Battery2 discharge energy(kWh) |
| `data.records.pv1Energy` | Double | yes |  | pv1 Energy |
| `data.records.pv2Energy` | Double | yes |  | pv2 Energy |
| `data.records.pv3Energy` | Double | yes |  | pv3 Energy |
| `data.records.pv4Energy` | Double | yes |  | pv4 Energy |
| `data.records.genEnergy` | Double | yes |  | generator Energy |
| `data.records.smartLoadEnergy` | Double | yes |  | Smart Load Energy |
| `data.records.offGridEnergy` | Double | yes |  | Back up load Energy |
| `data.records.gridTiedEnergy` | Double | yes |  | Home load Energy |
| `data.total` | number | yes | 110 | Total page |
| `data.size` | number | yes | 10 | Page size |
| `data.current` | number | yes | 1 | Current page |
| `data.orders` | object | yes | {} |  |
| `data.optimizeCountSql` | string | yes | true |  |
| `data.hitCount` | string | yes | false |  |
| `data.searchCount` | string | yes | true |  |
| `data.pages` | number | yes | 11 |  |

### Plant Energy data query for year, month, day

`GET /openApi/data/plantDataEnergy`  
_Status: Completed · Auth: inherit_

This endpoint adjusts the response data based on the timeDimension parameter. Depending on whether it's set to day, month, year, or total, the data values in the response vary accordingly.Energy response unit kWh precision to two decimal places

**Headers**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Authorization` | string | yes | Bearer_eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJkZW1vVXNlciI6ZmFsc2UsInN1YiI6 | authentication token  |

**Body parameters (form-data)**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `plantId` | string | yes | 020505004822242582 | Plant ID |
| `timeDimension` | string | yes | day | Query dimension :day month year total , when dimension  is total response all , dateStr can be empty |
| `dateStr` | string | no |  | Query time: (yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss) The dateStr, startTime, and endTime parameters are mutually exclusive  |
| `pageNum` | number | yes | 1 | Page number (default 1) |
| `pageSize` | number | yes | 10 | PageSize，The maximum value is 300 |
| `startTime` | string | yes |  | Query time: (yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss) The start time and end time cannot be transmitted separately    |
| `endTime` | string | yes |  | Query time: (yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss) The start time and end time cannot be transmitted separately   |

**Response — Success (200)**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `code` | number | yes | 200 | Code |
| `message` | string | yes | Success | Message |
| `data` | object | yes |  | Return Data |
| `data.records` | object | yes |  | records |
| `data.records.deviceSn` | string | yes | 020505004822242582 | Device serial number |
| `data.records.dataTime` | string | yes | 2023-04-03 | Data time |
| `data.records.timeStamp` | number | yes | 1680451200000 | Device time (timestamp) converted based on UTC+8 time zone |
| `data.records.gridInput` | Double | yes |  | Grid input Energy (kWh) |
| `data.records.feedOutput` | Double | yes |  | Grid feed Energy (kWh) |
| `data.records.generateEnergy` | Double | yes |  | Power generation |
| `data.records.inversionEnergy` | Double | yes |  | Inversion Energy |
| `data.records.batCharEnergy` | Double | yes |  | Battery charge energy(kWh) |
| `data.records.bat2ChartEnergy` | Double | yes |  | Battery2 charge energy(kWh) |

### Historical real-time data query (single device)

`GET /openApi/data/deviceDataHistory/{deviceSn}?dateStr=&pageNum=&pageSize=`  
_Status: Completed · Auth: inherit_

The application server can call this interface to query historical real-time data of a single device on the Fsolar platform.

For detailed explanation of response parameters, please refer to the following link
https://doc.apipost.net/docs/detail/2c65f6449c64000?target_id=81e8862bde00f

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dateStr` | string | yes |  | Date string, format: yyyy MM dd HH: mm: ss |
| `pageNum` | string | yes |  | Page number (default 1) |

### Event Query (alarms and faults)

`GET /openApi/data/deviceDataWarn/{deviceSn}`  
_Status: Completed · Auth: inherit_

The application server can call this interface to Query Events (alarms and faults) on the Fsolar platform.

If startTime and endTime are not provided, the default is to query alarm data from the past seven days.

**Headers**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Authorization` | string | yes |  | Authorization token |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `deviceSn` | string | yes |  | Device serial number |

**Body parameters (form-data)**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `startTime` | string | no |  | with the specified format is "Start Time" or "Beginning Time," formatted as "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss, The start time and end time cannot be transmitted separately only support 7 days  |
| `endTime` | string | no |  | with the specified format is "End Time," formatted as "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss The start time and end time cannot be transmitted separately  only support 7 days  |
| `state` | string | no |  | with the specified statuses is  0: Unprocessed 1: Processed 2: Expired 3: In Progress |

**Response — OK (200)**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `code` | integer | no |  | Code |
| `message` | string | no |  | Return Message |
| `data.dataTimeStr` | string | no |  | Event time |
| `data.deviceSn` | string | no |  | Device serial number |
| `data.timeZone` | string | no |  | Time Zone |
| `data.warnCode` | string | no |  | Event Code |
| `data.warringName` | string | no |  | Event Name |
| `data.warringType` | string | no |  | Event Type(F:fault, W:alarm) |

### The document provides an explanation of the differences in historical data for devices.

/openApi/data/deviceDataHistory/  (Historical real-time data query (single device))

/openApi/data/devicesDataHistory/ (Historical real-time data query (batch device))

|  acTOutFreq|Double|Backup Load L3 Frequency|Hz|
|  acROutPower|Double|Backup Load L1 Power|kW|
|  acSOutPower|Double|Backup Load L1 Power|kW|
|  acTOutPower|Double|Backup Load L1 Power|kW|
|  acTotalOutActPower|Double|"Total Backup Load Active Power|kW|
|  acTotalOutAppaPower|Double|"Total Backup Load Apparent Power|kVA|
|  meterPower|Double|Home Load|kW|
|  emsVoltage|Double|Battery Voltage|V|
|  emsVoltage2|Double|Battery2 Voltage|V|
|  emsCurrent|Double|Battery Current|A|
|  emsCurrent2|Double|Battery2 Current|A|
|  emsPower|Double|Battery Power|kW|
|  emsPower2|Double|Battery2 Power|kW|
|  emsSoc|Double|SOC|%|
|  emsSoc2|Double|SOC2|%|
|  genVoltage|Double|Gen L1 Voltage|V|

|  genVoltage3|Double|Gen L3 Voltage|V|
|  genCurrent|Double|Gen L1 Current|A|

|  genFrequency|Double|Gen L1 Frequency|Hz|

|  genTotalPower|Double|Gen Total Power|kW|

|  devTempMax|Double|Inverter Max Temp|℃|
|  devTempMin|Double|Inverter Min Temp|℃|

## Plant Interface

### Plants list

`GET /openApi/plants/list`  
_Status: Completed · Auth: inherit_

**Headers**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Authorization` | Text | yes | Bearer_eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJkZW1vVXNlciI6ZmFsc2UsInN1YiI6 | User token, which can be obtained from authentication and refreshing tokens. |

**Body parameters (form-data)**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `plantId` | number | no |  | Plant ID |
| `pageNum` | number | yes | 1 | Page number, starting from 1, mandatory. |
| `pageSize` | number | yes | 10 | Number of items per page, maximum value is 300, required. |
| `plantName` | string | no |  | Plant Name |

**Response — Success (200)**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `code` | number | yes | 200 | code, 200 successful response |
| `message` | string | yes | Success | message |
| `data` | object | yes |  | data |
| `data.dataList` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.dataList.deviceSn` | string | yes | 020505004822240024 | Device serial number |
| `data.dataList.masterVersion` | object | yes |  | Inverter - Control version, ps: This field only has numerical values for inverters |
| `data.dataList.slaveVersion` | object | yes |  | Inverter - From Control Version |
| `data.dataList.commVersion` | object | yes |  | Inverter - Communication version, ps: This field only has numerical values for inverters |
| `data.dataList.hardwareVersion` | object | yes |  | Inverter - Hardware version, ps: This field only has numerical values for inverters |
| `data.dataList.bpMasterVersion` | object | yes |  | Battery Pack - Control Version, ps: This field only has a numerical value for the battery pack |
| `data.dataList.bpIpaVersion` | object | yes |  | Battery pack IPA version, ps: This field only has a numerical value for the battery pack |
| `data.dataList.bpSubVersion` | object | yes |  | Battery Pack - Auxiliary Version, ps: This field only has a numerical value for the battery pack |
| `data.total` | string | yes | 6 | total page |
| `data.totalPage` | string | yes | 1 |  |
| `data.pageSize` | string | yes | 10 | The maximum number of entries per page is 300, which is required |
| `data.currentPage` | string | yes | 1 |  |
| `data.data` | object | yes |  | Additional data |

```json
{
  "code": 200,
  "message": "Success",
  "data": {
    "dataList": [
      {
        "plantId": "11614669775",
        "plantName": "电站A",
        "timeZone": "UTC+03:00",
        "plantAddress": "11",
        "plantAuthority": 1
      },
      {
        "plantId": "1514942003",
        "plantName": "电站B",
        "timeZone": "UTC+08:00",
        "plantAddress": "公司",
        "plantAuthority": 0
      },
      {
        "plantId": "15135515",
        "plantName": "电站C",
        "timeZone": "UTC+08:00",
        "plantAddress": "广州",
        "plantAuthority": 1
      }
    ],
    "pageSize": "10",
    "data": null
  }
}
```

_Status: Completed · Auth: inherit_

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Authorization` | Text | yes | Bearer_eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJkZW1vVXNlciI6ZmFsc2UsInN1YiI6 | User token, which can be obtained from authentication and refreshing tokens. |

```json
}
```

## Remote Control 

The application server can remotely control devices (inverters, etc.).

### Query Remote Control Setting Params

`GET /openApi/cmd/deviceSetting/params?typeCode=&subTypeCode=&version=`  
_Status: In Progress · Auth: inherit_

Query Remote Control Setting Params

**Headers**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Content-Type` | string | yes | application/x-www-form-urlencoded |  |
| `Authorization` | string | yes | Bearer_eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJkZW1vVXNlciI6ZmFsc2UsInN1YiI6 |  |
| `Lang` | string | yes | en_US |  |

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `typeCode` | string | yes |  | Device Type Code |
| `subTypeCode` | string | yes |  | Device Sub Type Code |
| `version` | string | no |  | Params Version (1.0.0/1.5.0) Default 1.0.0  |

**Response — Success (200)**

```json
{
  "code": 200,
  "message": "Success",
  "data": [
    {
      "fieldName": "fpOverFrequencyDeratingEnd",
      "definition": "UF_transition frequency",
      "rang": "40~70",
      "unit": "HZ",
      "required": false,
      "fieldType": "Number",
      "description": "F(P) Over Frequency Derating End"
    }
  ]
}
```

**Response — Fail (404)**


### Remote Control Setting

`POST /openApi/cmd/deviceSetting`  
_Status: Completed · Auth: inherit_

The application server can call this interface to set parameters for devices (inverters, etc.) through the Fsolar platform. Pay attention to the range of parameters, otherwise the parameters may not be set successfully.<br>
Default support T-REX-6KLP1G01、T-REX-10KHP3G01、T-REX-5KLP1G01、T-REX-10KLP3G01. <br>
More device support see: [Remote Control Setting Attachment](https://doc.apipost.net/docs/detail/2c65f6449c64000?target_id=a9a9d743de001)<br>

**Headers**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Content-Type` | string | yes | application/json |  |
| `Authorization` | string | yes | Bearer_eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJkZW1vVXNlciI6ZmFsc2UsInN1YiI6 | User token, which can be obtained from authentication and refreshing tokens. |
| `Lang` | string | yes | en_US | Language, currently supports English en_US. |

**Body parameters (json)**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `content.acOutputRatedFrequency` | integer | no | 1 | AC Output Rated Frequency，0：50Hz, 1：60Hz |
| `content.acOutputRatedVoltage` | number | no |  | AC Output Rated Voltage，Cannot be set |
| `content.antiIslandingDetectionEnable` | integer | no | 1 | Anti-Islanding Detection，0：Disable， 1：Enable |
| `content.batteryChargedVoltage` | number | no | 51.3 | Battery Charged Voltage，It is related to the "Battery Model",Each Model The range of values is 48~60V |
| `content.batteryFloatingChargedVoltage` | number | no | 52.5 | Battery Floating Charged Voltage，It is related to the "Battery Model",Each Model The range of values is 48~60V |
| `content.batteryMaxChargedCurrent` | number | no | 160 | Battery Max Charged Current，The range of values is 1~200A |
| `content.batteryMaxDischargeCurrent` | number | no | 110 | Battery Max Discharge Current，The range of values is 5~200A |
| `content.batteryModel` | integer | no | 1 | Battery Model，0：User defined，1：Lithium battery(default)，2：FelicitySolar(LPBF series)，3：FelicitySolar(LPBA series) |
| `content.batteryModules` | integer | no | 1 | Battery Modules，It is related to the "Battery Model", and can be set when the battery type is "User defined" |
| `content.batteryOffGridDischargeDepthSoc` | integer | no | 40 | Battery Off Grid Discharge Depth Soc，The range of values is 0~100% |
| `content.batteryOffGridRecoveryDepthSoc` | integer | no | 60 | Battery Off Grid Recovery Depth Soc，The range of values is 0~100% |
| `content.batteryOnGridDischargeDepthSoc` | integer | no | 50 | Battery On Grid Discharge DepthSoc，The range of values is 10~100% |
| `content.buzzerEnable` | integer | no | 1 | Buzzer Enable，0：Disable， 1：Enable |
| `content.commOffLineEnable` | integer | no | 0 | Comm Off Line Enable，0：Disable， 1：Enable |
| `content.deratingByVoltageEnable` | integer | no | 0 | 110%u Derating By Voltage Enable |
| `content.ecoRule1.daysOfEffectiveWeek.0` | array | no | ["MONDAY", "TUESDAY"] | ECO Mode Rule 1，Days Of Effective Week,Valuable：MONDAY, TUESDAY, WEDNESDAY, THURSDAY, FRIDAY, SATURDAY, SUNDAY，Multiple Choice |
| `content.ecoRule1.power` | integer | no | 100 | ECO Mode Rule 1，Battery charging or discharging power,Unit：W，The range of values is 0~ inverter rated power |
| `content.ecoRule1.ruleMode` | integer | no | 1 | ECO Mode Rule 1，0：disable, 1: enable charge 2: enable discharge |
| `content.ecoRule1.soc` | integer | no | 50 | ECO Mode Rule 1，Battery charging or discharging soc，Unit：%，The range of values is 0~100% |
| `content.ecoRule1.startDay` | string | no | 01:01 | ECO Mode Rule 1，Starting date, Format: mm:dd |
| `content.ecoRule1.startTime` | string | no | 14:20 | ECO Mode Rule 1，Starting time, Format: HH:ss |
| `content.ecoRule1.stopDay` | string | no | 12:12 | ECO Mode Rule 1，Stop date, Format: mm:dd |
| `content.ecoRule1.stopTime` | string | no | 23:59 | ECO Mode Rule 1，Stop time, Format: HH:ss |

**Response — OK (200)**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `code` | integer | no |  | code |
| `message` | string | no |  | message |
| `data.deviceSn` | string | no |  | Device serial number |
| `data.id` | integer | no |  | setting command id |
| `data` | object | no |  |  |

```json
{
  "code": 200,
  "message": "Success",
  "data": {
    "id": "9797479076789696",
    "deviceSn": "020505004822242582"
  }
}
```

**Response — token expired (200)**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `code` | string | yes | 998 |  |
| `message` | string | yes | The token has expired, please log in again |  |
| `data` | string | yes | {} |  |

```json
{
  "code": 998,
  "message": "The token has expired, please log in again",
  "data": {}
}
```

**Response — Permission Denied (200)**

```json
{
  "code": 2001528,
  "message": "Insufficient permissions",
  "data": "Insufficient permissions"
}
```

**Response — Failed (200)**

```json
{
  "code": 1006051,
  "message": "Failed to send command.",
  "data": "Failed to send command."
}
```

### Remote Control Setting Attachment (T-REX、IVGM)

#### T-REX
for T-REX-15KLP1G01、T-REX-8KLP1G01、T-REX-50KHP3G01, the request parameters as follows：

|Key|Name|Range|unit|Required|Type|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
|batteryMode|Bat Mode|0:Lithium<br>1:Use Batt V <br> 2:No bat||No|Number||
|batteryCapacity|Bat Capacity|0~500|AH|No|Number||
|batteryMaxChargedCurrent|Max A Charge|0~55|A|No|Number|Max battery charg current. Parallel bat1&bat2:if the inverter battery wiring ports are connect to one battery bank, please enable this function.|
|batteryMaxDischargeCurrent|Max A Discharge|0~55|A|No|Number|Max battery discharge current. Parallel bat1&bat2:if the inverter battery wiring ports are connect to one battery bank, please enable this function.|
|batteryParallelEnable|Parallel bat1&bat2|0:Stand Alone <br>1:Parallel||No|Number|Parallel bat1&bat2:if the inverter battery wiring ports are connect to one battery bank, please enable this function.|
|genAutoStartChargeSoc|GEN Auto Start Charge|0~100|%|No|Number|generator Auto Start Charge Soc|
|genAutoExitChargeSoc|GEN Exit Charge|5~100|%|No|Number|generator Auto Exit Charge Soc|
|gridChargeCurrent|Grid Charge Current|0~55|A|No|Number|grid Charge Current|
|genChargeCurrent|GEN Charge Current|0~55|A|No|Number|generator Charge Current|
|genStartSignalEnable|GEN Start Signal|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||No|Number|generator Start Signal Enable,0: Disable, 1: Enable|
|genChargeEnable|Grid Charge Enable|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||No|Number|It's allowed to use power fed from the grid port, which includes grid or generator connected to the grid port, to charge the battery.|
|gridChargeEnable|GEN Charge Enable|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||No|Number|Use the power of generator to charge the battery.|
|gridAutoStartChargeVoltage|Grid Auto Start Charge Voltage|150~800|V|No|Number||
|gridAutoExitChargeVoltage|Grid Exit Charge Voltage|150~800|V|No|Number||
|genAutoStartChargeVoltage|GEN Auto Start Charge Voltage|150~800|V|No|Number|generator Auto Start Charge Voltage|
|genAutoExitChargeVoltage|GEN Exit Charge Voltage|150~800|V|No|Number|generator Auto Exit Charge Voltage|
|genMaxRunTime|GEN Max Run Time|0~24|hours|No|Number|generator Max Run Time|
|lithiumProtocol|Lithium Protocol|0~20||No|Number||
|batteryFloatingChargedVoltage|Bat float voltage|150~800|V|No|Number|Battery full charge voltage.|
|batteryRestartOutputVoltage|Bat Restart|150~800|V|No|Number|battery Restart Output Voltage|
|batteryLowVoltageAlarmVoltage|Bat Low Alarm|150~800|V|No|Number|battery Low Voltage Alarm Voltage|
|batteryShutdownVoltage|Bat Shutdown|150~800|V|No|Number|battery Shutdown Voltage|
|batteryRestartOutputSoc|Bat Restart|0~100|%|No|Number|battery Restart Output Soc|
|batteryLowVoltageAlarmSoc|Bat Low Alarm SOC|0~100|%|No|Number|battery Low Voltage Alarm Soc|
|batteryShutdownSoc|Bat Shutdown SOC|0~100|%|No|Number|battery Shutdown Soc|
|operatedMode|System Mode|0:Selling Mode  <br> 1:Zero Export To Load  <br> 2:Zero Export To CT  <br> 3:Scheduling Mode||No|Number|Selling First: This Mode allows hybrid inverter to sell back any excess power produced by the solar panels to the grid. If Time Of Use is active, the battery energy also can be sold into grid. <br> The PV energy will be used to power the load and charge the battery and then excess energy will flow to grid. <br> Power source priority for the load is as follows: <br> 1. Solar Panels. <br> 2. Grid. when Energy Pattern tick Batt First. <br> Battery (until programable SOC discharge is reached). when Energy Pattern tick Load First and disable Grid charge. <br> Zero Export To Load: Hybrid inverter will only provide power to the backup load connected. The hybrid inverter will neither provide power to the home load nor sell power to grid. The built-in CT will detect power flowing back to the grid and will reduce the power of the inverter only to supply the backup load and charge the battery. <br> Zero Export To CT: Hybrid inverter will not only provide power to the backup load connected but also give power to the home load connected. If PV power and battery power is insufficient, it will take grid energy as supplement. The hybrid inverter will not sell power to grid. In this mode, a CT is needed. Please refer to manual CT connection for the installation method of CT. The external CT will detect power flowing back to the grid and will reduce the power of the inverter only to supply the backup load, charge battery and home load. <br> Scheduling mode: Based on system requirements and operating conditions, adjust the working status of the inverter and its coordinated operation with other devices through power allocation. Scheduling mode: Based on system requirements and operating conditions, adjust the working status of the inverter and its coordinated operation with other devices through power allocation"|
|zeroExportToLoadEnable|Solar Sell(to Load)|0:Disable  <br> 1:Enable||No|Number|“Solar sell” is supplement for Zero Export To Load or Zero Export To CT: when this item is active, the surplus PV energy can be sold back to grid too. When it is active, PV Power source priority usage is as follows: load consumption and charge battery and feed into grid.|
|zeroExportToCtEnable|Solar Sell(to CT)|0:Disable  <br> 1:Enable"||No|Number|“Solar sell” is supplement for Zero Export To Load or Zero Export To CT: when this item is active, the surplus PV energy can be sold back to grid too. When it is active, PV Power source priority usage is as follows: load consumption and charge battery and feed into grid.|
|dispatchActivePowerGiven|Active Power|-55~55|KW|No|Number|"The charge power(Active Power greater than 0) or discharge power(Active Power less than 0) in the Scheduling mode.Reactive power can be set at Gird Setting/Connect"|
|maxSellingPower|Max. Sell power|0~550|KW|No|Number|max Selling Power|
|zeroExportHysteresisPower|Zero-export Power|-5000~5000|W|No|Number|"For Zero Export To Load or Zero Export To CT, and the “Solar sell” is not active.  <br> It tells the grid output power threshold to ensure the hybrid inverter won' t feed power to grid."|
|energyPriority|Energy Priority|0:Bat First  <br> 1:Load First||No|Number|"Energy Pattern:Priority of PV power usage.  <br> Batt First: PV power is firstly used to charge the battery and then used to power the load. If PV power is insufficient, grid will make supplement for battery and load simultaneously. <br> Load First: PV power is firstly used to power the load and then used to charge the battery. If PV power is insufficient, Grid will provide power to load, but neither the battery power to load nor the Grid charge to battery."|
|gridPeakShavingEnable|Grid Peak Shaving Enable|0:Disable  <br> 1:Enable||No|Number|"Grid Peak Shaving: <br> 1. To use Peak-Shaving on a generator, the equipment MUST be connected to the “GRID” terminal of the inverter. <br> 2. Peak-Shaving helps reduce grid consumption during peak demand by utilizing battery backup power. It can also be used to prevent generator overload above a specified power threshold. <br> 3. Install the CT sensors on grid / generator lines L1, L2. The arrows on the CTs MUST point toward  the GRID. <br> 4. The T-REX INVERTER supplies power from the batteries whenever the “Power” threshold is met. <br> 5. This mode will automatically adjust the ""Grid Charge” amperage (A) to avoid generator overloads during battery charging. <br> 6. Grid Peak-Shaving will automatically enable “Time of Use” and MUST be configured."|
|gridPeakShavingPower|Grid Peak Shaving Power|0~550|KW|No|Number|grid Peak Shaving Power|
|timeOfUseEnable|Time Of Use|0:Disable  <br> 1:Enable||No|Number|"Time Of Use: it is used to program when to use grid or generator to charge the battery, and when to discharge the battery to power the load. Only tick ""Time Of Use"" then the follow items (Grid, charge, time, power etc.) will take effect. <br> Note: when tick Selling First and click Time Of Use, the battery power can be sold into grid.  <br> Charge Source: select grid or generator to charge the battery."|

#### IVGM100600
|Key|Name|Range|unit|Required|Type|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
|acOutputRatedVoltage|AC Output Rated Voltage|220:220V <br> 230:230V <br> 240:240V|V|NO|Number||
|acOutputRatedFrequency|AC Output Rated Frequency|0:50Hz <br> 1:60Hz|Hz|NO|Number||
|gridPowerUnbalanceEnable|Grid Power Unbalance|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||NO|Number||
|powerFactor|Power Factor|-0.99~-0.8,0.8~1||NO|Number||
|gridWaveformDetectionMode|Detection Mode|0:Wave Detect <br> 1:Half Wave Detect <br> 2:Disable Wave Detect||NO|Number||
|batteryModel|Battery Model|0:User defined <br> 1:Third party(Lithium battery) <br> 2:Felicity(LPBF/LUX-X series) <br> 3:Felicity(LPBA/LUX-Y/LUX-E  series)||NO|Number||
|batteryOnGridDischargeDepthSoc|Battery Discharged Depth(On-Grid, Bms Connected)|10~100|%|NO|Number|The battery SOC is lower than the battery on-grid discharge depth, and the battery stops discharging|
|batteryOffGridDischargeDepthSoc|Battery Discharged Depth(Off-Grid, Bms Connected)|0~100|%|NO|Number|The battery SOC is lower than the battery off-grid discharge depth, and the battery stops discharging|
|batteryOffGridRecoveryDepthSoc|Battery Restart Depth(Off-Grid, Bms Connected)|0~100|%|NO|Number|When the battery SOC is lower than the setting value of "Battery discharge depth (off grid)", the inverter can only be output off the grid again after the SOC recovers to the setting value higher than "Battery restart depth|
|lcdBacklightEnable|LCD Backlight|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||NO|Number||
|buzzerEnable|Buzzer|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||NO|Number||
|overLoadProtectionResetEnable|Over Load Protection Reset|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||NO|Number|The fault will be cleared automatically after the overload protection of the machine. If the overload protection occurs 3 times within half an hour, the overload fault will not be cleared|
|remoteOnOffEnable|Remote control|0:ON <br> 1:OFF||NO|Number|The machine shuts down and enters standby mode|
|remoteOutputOnOffControl|AC Output ON/OFF|0:ON <br> 1:OFF||NO|Number||
|zeroExportFunction|Zero Export Mode|1:To load <br> 2:To CT||NO|Number|"The feed power is limited according to the setting of ""grid connection power limit"".<br>1) To load:<br>The inverter can supply power to the backup load and sell power to the grid but the selling power will not exceed the setting value of ""grid connection power limit""<br>2) To CT:<br>The inverter can supply power to the backup load, household load and sell power to the grid, but the selling power will not exceed the setting value of ""grid connection power limit"".In this mode, a CT is needed."|
|zeroExportAdjustmentPower|Zero Export Power|-500~500|W|NO|Number|When the grid-connected power is set to 0, it is used to calibrate the sampling deviation to ensure that the inverter will not feed power to the grid|
|clearEnergyStorageLog|Clear Energy Storage Log|1:Clear||NO|Number||
|clearEventLog|Clear Event Log|1:Clear||NO|Number||
|factoryReset|Factory Reset|1:Reset||NO|Number||
|eventLogFunctionEnable|Event Log Function|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|faultLogFunctionEnable|Fault Log Function|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|gfciLevel1ProtectionEnable|GFCI One-level Protection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|gfciLevel2ProtectionEnable|GFCI Two-level Protection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|gfciLevel3ProtectionEnable|GFCI Three-level Protection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|isoDetectionEnable|ISO Detection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|antiIslandingDetectionEnable|Anti-Islanding Detection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|pvParallelSetting|PV Parallel Set|0:Stand Alone <br> 1:Parallel||NO|Number||
|fixQPencent|FixQ Pencent|-60~60|%|NO|Number||
|bmsHeartbeat|BMS Heartbeat|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number|After enabling, if the battery type is lithium battery and BMS communication fails, it will report BMS communication failure|
|operatedMode|Operated Mode|0:General Mode <br> 1:Backup Mode <br> 2:Eco Mode <br> 3:Gen Mode||NO|Number|"Set inverter operation mode:<br>1) General mode:<br>In this mode, the load is mainly powered by PV and battery.<br>2) Backup mode:<br>In this mode, PV gives priority to charging the battery to ensure that the battery is always full.<br>3) ECO mode:<br>In this mode, you can set a specific time to take power from the grid to charge the battery or use the power of the grid to charge the battery, and operate in general mode outside the set time.<br>4) Generator mode:<br>In this mode, the load is mainly powered by PV and battery."|
|ecoRule1.ruleMode|ECO Mode 1|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge  <br> 3:AC Couple||NO|Number|"Economic model rules:<br>1) Disable: No charging and discharging<br>2) Charge: Charge the battery from the grid<br>3) Discharge: Selling power from battery to the grid<br>4)AC Couple:Spontaneous self-use, waste electricity Internet access"|

|ecoRule1.startDay|Start Day1|MM:1~12<br> dd:1~31||NO|String|Starting day, Format: MM:dd|
|ecoRule1.stopDay|Stop Day1|MM:1~12<br> dd:1~31||NO|String|Stop day, Format: MM:dd|

#### IVGM10048
|Key|Name|Range|unit|Required|Type|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

|powerFactor|Power Factor|-0.99~-0.8,0.8~1||NO|Number||
|gridWaveformDetectionMode|Detection Mode|0:Wave Detect <br> 1:Half Wave Detect <br> 2:Disable Wave Detect||NO|Number||
|batteryModel|Battery Model|0:User defined <br> 1:Third party(Lithium battery) <br> 2:Felicity(LPBF/LUX-X series) <br> 3:Felicity(LPBA/LUX-Y/LUX-E  series)||NO|Number||
|batteryOnGridDischargeDepthSoc|Battery Discharged Depth(On-Grid, Bms Connected)|10~100|%|NO|Number|The battery SOC is lower than the battery on-grid discharge depth, and the battery stops discharging|
|batteryOffGridDischargeDepthSoc|Battery Discharged Depth(Off-Grid, Bms Connected)|0~100|%|NO|Number|The battery SOC is lower than the battery off-grid discharge depth, and the battery stops discharging|
|batteryOffGridRecoveryDepthSoc|Battery Restart Depth(Off-Grid, Bms Connected)|0~100|%|NO|Number|When the battery SOC is lower than the setting value of "Battery discharge depth (off grid)", the inverter can only be output off the grid again after the SOC recovers to the setting value higher than "Battery restart depth|
|lcdBacklightEnable|LCD Backlight|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||NO|Number||
|buzzerEnable|Buzzer|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||NO|Number||
|overLoadProtectionResetEnable|Over Load Protection Reset|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||NO|Number|The fault will be cleared automatically after the overload protection of the machine. If the overload protection occurs 3 times within half an hour, the overload fault will not be cleared|
|remoteOnOffEnable|Remote control|0:ON <br> 1:OFF||NO|Number|The machine shuts down and enters standby mode|
|remoteOutputOnOffControl|AC Output ON/OFF|0:ON <br> 1:OFF||NO|Number||
|zeroExportFunction|Zero Export Mode|1:To load <br> 2:To CT||NO|Number|"The feed power is limited according to the setting of ""grid connection power limit"".<br>1) To load:<br>The inverter can supply power to the backup load and sell power to the grid but the selling power will not exceed the setting value of ""grid connection power limit""<br>2) To CT:<br>The inverter can supply power to the backup load, household load and sell power to the grid, but the selling power will not exceed the setting value of ""grid connection power limit"".In this mode, a CT is needed."|
|zeroExportAdjustmentPower|Zero Export Power|-500~500|W|NO|Number|When the grid-connected power is set to 0, it is used to calibrate the sampling deviation to ensure that the inverter will not feed power to the grid|
|clearEnergyStorageLog|Clear Energy Storage Log|1:Clear||NO|Number||
|clearEventLog|Clear Event Log|1:Clear||NO|Number||
|factoryReset|Factory Reset|1:Reset||NO|Number||
|eventLogFunctionEnable|Event Log Function|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|faultLogFunctionEnable|Fault Log Function|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|gfciLevel1ProtectionEnable|GFCI One-level Protection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|gfciLevel2ProtectionEnable|GFCI Two-level Protection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|gfciLevel3ProtectionEnable|GFCI Three-level Protection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|isoDetectionEnable|ISO Detection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|antiIslandingDetectionEnable|Anti-Islanding Detection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|pvParallelSetting|PV Parallel Set|0:Stand Alone <br> 1:Parallel||NO|Number||
|fixQPencent|FixQ Pencent|-60~60|%|NO|Number||
|bmsHeartbeat|BMS Heartbeat|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number|After enabling, if the battery type is lithium battery and BMS communication fails, it will report BMS communication failure|
|operatedMode|Operated Mode|0:General Mode <br> 1:Backup Mode <br> 2:Eco Mode <br> 3:Gen Mode||NO|Number|"Set inverter operation mode:<br>1) General mode:<br>In this mode, the load is mainly powered by PV and battery.<br>2) Backup mode:<br>In this mode, PV gives priority to charging the battery to ensure that the battery is always full.<br>3) ECO mode:<br>In this mode, you can set a specific time to take power from the grid to charge the battery or use the power of the grid to charge the battery, and operate in general mode outside the set time.<br>4) Generator mode:<br>In this mode, the load is mainly powered by PV and battery."|
|ecoRule1.ruleMode|ECO Mode 1|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge  <br> 3:AC Couple||NO|Number|"Economic model rules:<br>1) Disable: No charging and discharging<br>2) Charge: Charge the battery from the grid<br>3) Discharge: Selling power from battery to the grid<br>4)AC Couple:Spontaneous self-use, waste electricity Internet access"|

#### IVGM6048
|Key|Name|Range|unit|Required|Type|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
|acOutputRatedVoltage|AC Output Rated Voltage|220:220V <br> 230:230V <br> 240:240V|V|NO|Number||
|acOutputRatedFrequency|AC Output Rated Frequency|0:50Hz <br> 1:60Hz|Hz|NO|Number||
|gridPowerUnbalanceEnable|Grid Power Unbalance|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||NO|Number||
|powerFactor|Power Factor|-0.99~-0.8,0.8~1||NO|Number||
|gridWaveformDetectionMode|Detection Mode|0:Wave Detect <br> 1:Half Wave Detect <br> 2:Disable Wave Detect||NO|Number||
|batteryModel|Battery Model|0:User defined <br> 1:Third party(Lithium battery) <br> 2:Felicity(LPBF/LUX-X series) <br> 3:Felicity(LPBA/LUX-Y/LUX-E  series)||NO|Number||
|batteryOnGridDischargeDepthSoc|Battery Discharged Depth(On-Grid, Bms Connected)|10~100|%|NO|Number|The battery SOC is lower than the battery on-grid discharge depth, and the battery stops discharging|
|batteryOffGridDischargeDepthSoc|Battery Discharged Depth(Off-Grid, Bms Connected)|0~100|%|NO|Number|The battery SOC is lower than the battery off-grid discharge depth, and the battery stops discharging|
|batteryOffGridRecoveryDepthSoc|Battery Restart Depth(Off-Grid, Bms Connected)|0~100|%|NO|Number|When the battery SOC is lower than the setting value of "Battery discharge depth (off grid)", the inverter can only be output off the grid again after the SOC recovers to the setting value higher than "Battery restart depth|
|lcdBacklightEnable|LCD Backlight|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||NO|Number||
|buzzerEnable|Buzzer|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||NO|Number||
|overLoadProtectionResetEnable|Over Load Protection Reset|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||NO|Number|The fault will be cleared automatically after the overload protection of the machine. If the overload protection occurs 3 times within half an hour, the overload fault will not be cleared|
|remoteOnOffEnable|Remote control|0:ON <br> 1:OFF||NO|Number|The machine shuts down and enters standby mode|
|remoteOutputOnOffControl|AC Output ON/OFF|0:ON <br> 1:OFF||NO|Number||
|zeroExportFunction|Zero Export Mode|1:To load <br> 2:To CT||NO|Number|"The feed power is limited according to the setting of ""grid connection power limit"". <br> 1) To load: <br> The inverter can supply power to the backup load and sell power to the grid but the selling power will not exceed the setting value of ""grid connection power limit"" <br> 2) To CT: <br> The inverter can supply power to the backup load, household load and sell power to the grid, but the selling power will not exceed the setting value of ""grid connection power limit"".In this mode, a CT is needed."|
|zeroExportAdjustmentPower|Zero Export Power|-500~500|W|NO|Number|When the grid-connected power is set to 0, it is used to calibrate the sampling deviation to ensure that the inverter will not feed power to the grid|
|clearEnergyStorageLog|Clear Energy Storage Log|1:Clear||NO|Number||
|clearEventLog|Clear Event Log|1:Clear||NO|Number||
|factoryReset|Factory Reset|1:Reset||NO|Number||
|eventLogFunctionEnable|Event Log Function|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|faultLogFunctionEnable|Fault Log Function|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|gfciLevel1ProtectionEnable|GFCI One-level Protection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|gfciLevel2ProtectionEnable|GFCI Two-level Protection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|gfciLevel3ProtectionEnable|GFCI Three-level Protection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|isoDetectionEnable|ISO Detection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|antiIslandingDetectionEnable|Anti-Islanding Detection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|pvParallelSetting|PV Parallel Set|0:Stand Alone <br> 1:Parallel||NO|Number||
|fixQPencent|FixQ Pencent|-60~60|%|NO|Number||
|bmsHeartbeat|BMS Heartbeat|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number|After enabling, if the battery type is lithium battery and BMS communication fails, it will report BMS communication failure|
|operatedMode|Operated Mode|0:General Mode <br> 1:Backup Mode <br> 2:Eco Mode <br> 3:Gen Mode||NO|Number|"Set inverter operation mode: <br> 1) General mode: <br> In this mode, the load is mainly powered by PV and battery. <br> 2) Backup mode: <br> In this mode, PV gives priority to charging the battery to ensure that the battery is always full. <br> 3) ECO mode: <br> In this mode, you can set a specific time to take power from the grid to charge the battery or use the power of the grid to charge the battery, and operate in general mode outside the set time. <br> 4) Generator mode: <br> In this mode, the load is mainly powered by PV and battery."|
|ecoRule1.ruleMode|ECO Mode 1|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge  <br> 3:AC Couple||NO|Number|"Economic model rules: <br> 1) Disable: No charging and discharging <br> 2) Charge: Charge the battery from the grid <br> 3) Discharge: Selling power from battery to the grid <br> 4)AC Couple:Spontaneous self-use, waste electricity Internet access"|

#### IVGM5048
|Key|Name|Range|unit|Required|Type|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
|acOutputRatedVoltage|AC Output Rated Voltage|220:220V <br> 230:230V <br> 240:240V|V|NO|Number||
|acOutputRatedFrequency|AC Output Rated Frequency|0:50Hz <br> 1:60Hz|Hz|NO|Number||
|gridPowerUnbalanceEnable|Grid Power Unbalance|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||NO|Number||
|powerFactor|Power Factor|-0.99~-0.8,0.8~1||NO|Number||
|gridWaveformDetectionMode|Detection Mode|0:Wave Detect <br> 1:Half Wave Detect <br> 2:Disable Wave Detect||NO|Number||
|batteryModel|Battery Model|0:User defined <br> 1:Third party(Lithium battery) <br> 2:Felicity(LPBF/LUX-X series) <br> 3:Felicity(LPBA/LUX-Y/LUX-E  series)||NO|Number||
|batteryOnGridDischargeDepthSoc|Battery Discharged Depth(On-Grid, Bms Connected)|10~100|%|NO|Number|The battery SOC is lower than the battery on-grid discharge depth, and the battery stops discharging|
|batteryOffGridDischargeDepthSoc|Battery Discharged Depth(Off-Grid, Bms Connected)|0~100|%|NO|Number|The battery SOC is lower than the battery off-grid discharge depth, and the battery stops discharging|
|batteryOffGridRecoveryDepthSoc|Battery Restart Depth(Off-Grid, Bms Connected)|0~100|%|NO|Number|When the battery SOC is lower than the setting value of "Battery discharge depth (off grid)", the inverter can only be output off the grid again after the SOC recovers to the setting value higher than "Battery restart depth|
|lcdBacklightEnable|LCD Backlight|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||NO|Number||
|buzzerEnable|Buzzer|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||NO|Number||
|overLoadProtectionResetEnable|Over Load Protection Reset|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||NO|Number|The fault will be cleared automatically after the overload protection of the machine. If the overload protection occurs 3 times within half an hour, the overload fault will not be cleared|
|remoteOnOffEnable|Remote control|0:ON <br> 1:OFF||NO|Number|The machine shuts down and enters standby mode|
|remoteOutputOnOffControl|AC Output ON/OFF|0:ON <br> 1:OFF||NO|Number||
|zeroExportFunction|Zero Export Mode|1:To load <br> 2:To CT||NO|Number|"The feed power is limited according to the setting of ""grid connection power limit"". <br> 1) To load: <br> The inverter can supply power to the backup load and sell power to the grid but the selling power will not exceed the setting value of ""grid connection power limit"" <br> 2) To CT: <br> The inverter can supply power to the backup load, household load and sell power to the grid, but the selling power will not exceed the setting value of ""grid connection power limit"".In this mode, a CT is needed."|
|zeroExportAdjustmentPower|Zero Export Power|-500~500|W|NO|Number|When the grid-connected power is set to 0, it is used to calibrate the sampling deviation to ensure that the inverter will not feed power to the grid|
|clearEnergyStorageLog|Clear Energy Storage Log|1:Clear||NO|Number||
|clearEventLog|Clear Event Log|1:Clear||NO|Number||
|factoryReset|Factory Reset|1:Reset||NO|Number||
|eventLogFunctionEnable|Event Log Function|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|faultLogFunctionEnable|Fault Log Function|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|gfciLevel1ProtectionEnable|GFCI One-level Protection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|gfciLevel2ProtectionEnable|GFCI Two-level Protection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|gfciLevel3ProtectionEnable|GFCI Three-level Protection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|isoDetectionEnable|ISO Detection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|antiIslandingDetectionEnable|Anti-Islanding Detection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|pvParallelSetting|PV Parallel Set|0:Stand Alone <br> 1:Parallel||NO|Number||
|fixQPencent|FixQ Pencent|-60~60|%|NO|Number||
|bmsHeartbeat|BMS Heartbeat|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number|After enabling, if the battery type is lithium battery and BMS communication fails, it will report BMS communication failure|
|operatedMode|Operated Mode|0:General Mode <br> 1:Backup Mode <br> 2:Eco Mode <br> 3:Gen Mode||NO|Number|"Set inverter operation mode: <br> 1) General mode: <br> In this mode, the load is mainly powered by PV and battery. <br> 2) Backup mode: <br> In this mode, PV gives priority to charging the battery to ensure that the battery is always full. <br> 3) ECO mode: <br> In this mode, you can set a specific time to take power from the grid to charge the battery or use the power of the grid to charge the battery, and operate in general mode outside the set time. <br> 4) Generator mode: <br> In this mode, the load is mainly powered by PV and battery."|
|ecoRule1.ruleMode|ECO Mode 1|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge  <br> 3:AC Couple||NO|Number|"Economic model rules: <br> 1) Disable: No charging and discharging <br> 2) Charge: Charge the battery from the grid <br> 3) Discharge: Selling power from battery to the grid <br> 4)AC Couple:Spontaneous self-use, waste electricity Internet access"|

|ecoRule1.stopTime|Stop Time1|HH:0~23<br> mm:0~59||NO|String|Stop time, Format: HH:mm|

|ecoRule1.stopDay|Stop Day1|MM:1~12<br> dd:1~31||NO|String|Stop day, Format: MM:dd|

|ecoRule1.soc|SOC1|0~100|%|NO|Number||
|ecoRule1.voltage|Voltage1|48~60|V|NO|Number|"1) Charging: Stop charging voltage. Battery voltage above this value stops charging (usually set to 57.6v for a single battery). <br> 2) Discharge: Stop discharge voltage. If the battery voltage is lower than this value, it will stop discharging."|

**Request Body Example.**


```json
{
	
    "batteryMode":1,
    "ecoRule1": {
        "soc":56,
        "startTime":"10:07",
        "stopTime":"13:05",
        "power":30,
        "daysOfEffectiveWeek":["MONDAY", "TUESDAY"]
    },
   
    "touEffectiveWeek":["MONDAY", "TUESDAY"],
	"deviceSn": "013548202500010000"
}
     
```

### Query Remote Control Setting value

`GET /openApi/cmd/deviceSetting/{deviceSn}`  
_Status: Completed · Auth: inherit_

The application server can call this interface to remotely query parameters of devices (inverters, etc.) through the Fsolar platform.<br>
Default support T-REX-6KLP1G01、T-REX-10KHP3G01、T-REX-5KLP1G01、T-REX-10KLP3G01. <br>
More device support see: [Query Remote Control Setting Attachment](https://doc.apipost.net/docs/detail/2c65f6449c64000?target_id=aa50fdb3c4003)<br>

**Headers**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Content-Type` | string | yes | application/x-www-form-urlencoded |  |
| `Authorization` | string | yes | Bearer_eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJkZW1vVXNlciI6ZmFsc2UsInN1YiI6 | User token, which can be obtained from authentication and refreshing tokens. |
| `Lang` | string | yes | en_US | Language, currently supports English en_US. |

**Path parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `deviceSn` | string | yes |  | deviceSn |

**Body parameters (form-data)**

| Name | Type | Required | Example | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `deviceSn` | string | yes |  | Device SN |

**Response — OK (200)**

|---|---|---|---|---|

| `message` | string | no |  | message |

### Query Remote Control Setting Attachment

#### T-REX
for T-REX-15KLP1G01、T-REX-8KLP1G01、T-REX-50KHP3G01, the request parameters as follows：

|Key|Name|Range|unit|Required|Type|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
|batteryMode|Bat Mode|0:Lithium<br>1:Use Batt V <br> 2:No bat||No|Number||
|batteryCapacity|Bat Capacity|0~500|AH|No|Number||
|batteryMaxChargedCurrent|Max A Charge|0~55|A|No|Number|Max battery charg current. Parallel bat1&bat2:if the inverter battery wiring ports are connect to one battery bank, please enable this function.|
|batteryMaxDischargeCurrent|Max A Discharge|0~55|A|No|Number|Max battery discharge current. Parallel bat1&bat2:if the inverter battery wiring ports are connect to one battery bank, please enable this function.|
|batteryParallelEnable|Parallel bat1&bat2|0:Stand Alone <br>1:Parallel||No|Number|Parallel bat1&bat2:if the inverter battery wiring ports are connect to one battery bank, please enable this function.|
|genAutoStartChargeSoc|GEN Auto Start Charge|0~100|%|No|Number|generator Auto Start Charge Soc|
|genAutoExitChargeSoc|GEN Exit Charge|5~100|%|No|Number|generator Auto Exit Charge Soc|
|gridChargeCurrent|Grid Charge Current|0~55|A|No|Number|grid Charge Current|
|genChargeCurrent|GEN Charge Current|0~55|A|No|Number|generator Charge Current|
|genStartSignalEnable|GEN Start Signal|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||No|Number|generator Start Signal Enable,0: Disable, 1: Enable|
|genChargeEnable|Grid Charge Enable|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||No|Number|It's allowed to use power fed from the grid port, which includes grid or generator connected to the grid port, to charge the battery.|
|gridChargeEnable|GEN Charge Enable|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||No|Number|Use the power of generator to charge the battery.|
|gridAutoStartChargeVoltage|Grid Auto Start Charge Voltage|150~800|V|No|Number||
|gridAutoExitChargeVoltage|Grid Exit Charge Voltage|150~800|V|No|Number||
|genAutoStartChargeVoltage|GEN Auto Start Charge Voltage|150~800|V|No|Number|generator Auto Start Charge Voltage|
|genAutoExitChargeVoltage|GEN Exit Charge Voltage|150~800|V|No|Number|generator Auto Exit Charge Voltage|
|genMaxRunTime|GEN Max Run Time|0~24|hours|No|Number|generator Max Run Time|
|lithiumProtocol|Lithium Protocol|0~20||No|Number||
|batteryFloatingChargedVoltage|Bat float voltage|150~800|V|No|Number|Battery full charge voltage.|
|batteryRestartOutputVoltage|Bat Restart|150~800|V|No|Number|battery Restart Output Voltage|
|batteryLowVoltageAlarmVoltage|Bat Low Alarm|150~800|V|No|Number|battery Low Voltage Alarm Voltage|
|batteryShutdownVoltage|Bat Shutdown|150~800|V|No|Number|battery Shutdown Voltage|
|batteryRestartOutputSoc|Bat Restart|0~100|%|No|Number|battery Restart Output Soc|
|batteryLowVoltageAlarmSoc|Bat Low Alarm SOC|0~100|%|No|Number|battery Low Voltage Alarm Soc|
|batteryShutdownSoc|Bat Shutdown SOC|0~100|%|No|Number|battery Shutdown Soc|
|operatedMode|System Mode|0:Selling Mode  <br> 1:Zero Export To Load  <br> 2:Zero Export To CT  <br> 3:Scheduling Mode||No|Number|Selling First: This Mode allows hybrid inverter to sell back any excess power produced by the solar panels to the grid. If Time Of Use is active, the battery energy also can be sold into grid. <br> The PV energy will be used to power the load and charge the battery and then excess energy will flow to grid. <br> Power source priority for the load is as follows: <br> 1. Solar Panels. <br> 2. Grid. when Energy Pattern tick Batt First. <br> Battery (until programable SOC discharge is reached). when Energy Pattern tick Load First and disable Grid charge. <br> Zero Export To Load: Hybrid inverter will only provide power to the backup load connected. The hybrid inverter will neither provide power to the home load nor sell power to grid. The built-in CT will detect power flowing back to the grid and will reduce the power of the inverter only to supply the backup load and charge the battery. <br> Zero Export To CT: Hybrid inverter will not only provide power to the backup load connected but also give power to the home load connected. If PV power and battery power is insufficient, it will take grid energy as supplement. The hybrid inverter will not sell power to grid. In this mode, a CT is needed. Please refer to manual CT connection for the installation method of CT. The external CT will detect power flowing back to the grid and will reduce the power of the inverter only to supply the backup load, charge battery and home load. <br> Scheduling mode: Based on system requirements and operating conditions, adjust the working status of the inverter and its coordinated operation with other devices through power allocation. Scheduling mode: Based on system requirements and operating conditions, adjust the working status of the inverter and its coordinated operation with other devices through power allocation"|
|zeroExportToLoadEnable|Solar Sell(to Load)|0:Disable  <br> 1:Enable||No|Number|“Solar sell” is supplement for Zero Export To Load or Zero Export To CT: when this item is active, the surplus PV energy can be sold back to grid too. When it is active, PV Power source priority usage is as follows: load consumption and charge battery and feed into grid.|
|zeroExportToCtEnable|Solar Sell(to CT)|0:Disable  <br> 1:Enable"||No|Number|“Solar sell” is supplement for Zero Export To Load or Zero Export To CT: when this item is active, the surplus PV energy can be sold back to grid too. When it is active, PV Power source priority usage is as follows: load consumption and charge battery and feed into grid.|
|dispatchActivePowerGiven|Active Power|-55~55|KW|No|Number|"The charge power(Active Power greater than 0) or discharge power(Active Power less than 0) in the Scheduling mode.Reactive power can be set at Gird Setting/Connect"|
|maxSellingPower|Max. Sell power|0~550|KW|No|Number|max Selling Power|
|zeroExportHysteresisPower|Zero-export Power|-5000~5000|W|No|Number|"For Zero Export To Load or Zero Export To CT, and the “Solar sell” is not active.  <br> It tells the grid output power threshold to ensure the hybrid inverter won' t feed power to grid."|
|energyPriority|Energy Priority|0:Bat First  <br> 1:Load First||No|Number|"Energy Pattern:Priority of PV power usage.  <br> Batt First: PV power is firstly used to charge the battery and then used to power the load. If PV power is insufficient, grid will make supplement for battery and load simultaneously. <br> Load First: PV power is firstly used to power the load and then used to charge the battery. If PV power is insufficient, Grid will provide power to load, but neither the battery power to load nor the Grid charge to battery."|
|gridPeakShavingEnable|Grid Peak Shaving Enable|0:Disable  <br> 1:Enable||No|Number|"Grid Peak Shaving: <br> 1. To use Peak-Shaving on a generator, the equipment MUST be connected to the “GRID” terminal of the inverter. <br> 2. Peak-Shaving helps reduce grid consumption during peak demand by utilizing battery backup power. It can also be used to prevent generator overload above a specified power threshold. <br> 3. Install the CT sensors on grid / generator lines L1, L2. The arrows on the CTs MUST point toward  the GRID. <br> 4. The T-REX INVERTER supplies power from the batteries whenever the “Power” threshold is met. <br> 5. This mode will automatically adjust the ""Grid Charge” amperage (A) to avoid generator overloads during battery charging. <br> 6. Grid Peak-Shaving will automatically enable “Time of Use” and MUST be configured."|
|gridPeakShavingPower|Grid Peak Shaving Power|0~550|KW|No|Number|grid Peak Shaving Power|
|timeOfUseEnable|Time Of Use|0:Disable  <br> 1:Enable||No|Number|"Time Of Use: it is used to program when to use grid or generator to charge the battery, and when to discharge the battery to power the load. Only tick ""Time Of Use"" then the follow items (Grid, charge, time, power etc.) will take effect. <br> Note: when tick Selling First and click Time Of Use, the battery power can be sold into grid.  <br> Charge Source: select grid or generator to charge the battery."|
|ecoRule1.gridChangingEnable|Grid Changing|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||No|Number|Grid Changing1: Time Of Use Rule 1, grid Changing Enable, 0: Disable, 1: Enable|
|ecoRule1.genChangingEnable|Gen Changing|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||No|Number|Gen Changing1: Time Of Use Rule 1, gen Changing Enable, 0: Disable, 1: Enable|
|ecoRule1.startTime|Start Time|HH:0~23 <br> mm:0~59||No|String|Start Time1: Time Of Use Rule 1, Starting time, Format: HH:mm|
|ecoRule1.stopTime|End Time|HH:0~23 <br> mm:0~59||No|String|End Time1: Time Of Use Rule 1, Stop time, Format: HH:mm|

|fpOverFrequencyDeratingRatio|Droop Over F|-500~500|%HZ|No|Number|"Decreases the power percentage of nominal power per Hz. For example, “Start Over F=50.2Hz, Stop Over F=51.2 Hz, Droop Over F =40%PE/Hz” ,define the current Grid Frequency is Fg, when the grid frequency reaches 50.2Hz, the inverter will decrease its active power at Droop of 40%. <br>The total decrease active power=( Fg- Start Over F) * Droop Over F *Pn. when grid frequency is larger than 51.2Hz, the active power will stop decreasing."|

|zeroExportFunction|Zero Export Mode|1:To load <br> 2:To CT||NO|Number|"The feed power is limited according to the setting of ""grid connection power limit"".<br>1) To load:<br>The inverter can supply power to the backup load and sell power to the grid but the selling power will not exceed the setting value of ""grid connection power limit""<br>2) To CT:<br>The inverter can supply power to the backup load, household load and sell power to the grid, but the selling power will not exceed the setting value of ""grid connection power limit"".In this mode, a CT is needed."|

|operatedMode|Operated Mode|0:General Mode <br> 1:Backup Mode <br> 2:Eco Mode <br> 3:Gen Mode||NO|Number|"Set inverter operation mode:<br>1) General mode:<br>In this mode, the load is mainly powered by PV and battery.<br>2) Backup mode:<br>In this mode, PV gives priority to charging the battery to ensure that the battery is always full.<br>3) ECO mode:<br>In this mode, you can set a specific time to take power from the grid to charge the battery or use the power of the grid to charge the battery, and operate in general mode outside the set time.<br>4) Generator mode:<br>In this mode, the load is mainly powered by PV and battery."|

|zeroExportFunction|Zero Export Mode|1:To load <br> 2:To CT||NO|Number|"The feed power is limited according to the setting of ""grid connection power limit"".<br>1) To load:<br>The inverter can supply power to the backup load and sell power to the grid but the selling power will not exceed the setting value of ""grid connection power limit""<br>2) To CT:<br>The inverter can supply power to the backup load, household load and sell power to the grid, but the selling power will not exceed the setting value of ""grid connection power limit"".In this mode, a CT is needed."|
|zeroExportAdjustmentPower|Zero Export Power|-500~500|W|NO|Number|When the grid-connected power is set to 0, it is used to calibrate the sampling deviation to ensure that the inverter will not feed power to the grid|
|clearEnergyStorageLog|Clear Energy Storage Log|1:Clear||NO|Number||
|clearEventLog|Clear Event Log|1:Clear||NO|Number||
|factoryReset|Factory Reset|1:Reset||NO|Number||
|eventLogFunctionEnable|Event Log Function|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|faultLogFunctionEnable|Fault Log Function|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|gfciLevel1ProtectionEnable|GFCI One-level Protection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|gfciLevel2ProtectionEnable|GFCI Two-level Protection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|gfciLevel3ProtectionEnable|GFCI Three-level Protection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|isoDetectionEnable|ISO Detection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|antiIslandingDetectionEnable|Anti-Islanding Detection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|pvParallelSetting|PV Parallel Set|0:Stand Alone <br> 1:Parallel||NO|Number||
|fixQPencent|FixQ Pencent|-60~60|%|NO|Number||
|bmsHeartbeat|BMS Heartbeat|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number|After enabling, if the battery type is lithium battery and BMS communication fails, it will report BMS communication failure|
|operatedMode|Operated Mode|0:General Mode <br> 1:Backup Mode <br> 2:Eco Mode <br> 3:Gen Mode||NO|Number|"Set inverter operation mode:<br>1) General mode:<br>In this mode, the load is mainly powered by PV and battery.<br>2) Backup mode:<br>In this mode, PV gives priority to charging the battery to ensure that the battery is always full.<br>3) ECO mode:<br>In this mode, you can set a specific time to take power from the grid to charge the battery or use the power of the grid to charge the battery, and operate in general mode outside the set time.<br>4) Generator mode:<br>In this mode, the load is mainly powered by PV and battery."|
|ecoRule1.ruleMode|ECO Mode 1|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge  <br> 3:AC Couple||NO|Number|"Economic model rules:<br>1) Disable: No charging and discharging<br>2) Charge: Charge the battery from the grid<br>3) Discharge: Selling power from battery to the grid<br>4)AC Couple:Spontaneous self-use, waste electricity Internet access"|
|ecoRule1.startTime|Start Time1|HH:0~23<br> mm:0~59||NO|String|Starting time, Format: HH:mm|
|ecoRule1.stopTime|Stop Time1|HH:0~23<br> mm:0~59||NO|String|Stop time, Format: HH:mm|

|ecoRule1.voltage|Voltage1|48~60|V|NO|Number|"1) Charging: Stop charging voltage. Battery voltage above this value stops charging (usually set to 57.6v for a single battery).<br>2) Discharge: Stop discharge voltage. If the battery voltage is lower than this value, it will stop discharging."|

#### IVGM6048
|Key|Name|Range|unit|Required|Type|Description|
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

|powerFactor|Power Factor|-0.99~-0.8,0.8~1||NO|Number||
|gridWaveformDetectionMode|Detection Mode|0:Wave Detect <br> 1:Half Wave Detect <br> 2:Disable Wave Detect||NO|Number||
|batteryModel|Battery Model|0:User defined <br> 1:Third party(Lithium battery) <br> 2:Felicity(LPBF/LUX-X series) <br> 3:Felicity(LPBA/LUX-Y/LUX-E  series)||NO|Number||
|batteryOnGridDischargeDepthSoc|Battery Discharged Depth(On-Grid, Bms Connected)|10~100|%|NO|Number|The battery SOC is lower than the battery on-grid discharge depth, and the battery stops discharging|
|batteryOffGridDischargeDepthSoc|Battery Discharged Depth(Off-Grid, Bms Connected)|0~100|%|NO|Number|The battery SOC is lower than the battery off-grid discharge depth, and the battery stops discharging|
|batteryOffGridRecoveryDepthSoc|Battery Restart Depth(Off-Grid, Bms Connected)|0~100|%|NO|Number|When the battery SOC is lower than the setting value of "Battery discharge depth (off grid)", the inverter can only be output off the grid again after the SOC recovers to the setting value higher than "Battery restart depth|
|lcdBacklightEnable|LCD Backlight|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||NO|Number||
|buzzerEnable|Buzzer|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||NO|Number||
|overLoadProtectionResetEnable|Over Load Protection Reset|0:Disable <br> 1:Enable||NO|Number|The fault will be cleared automatically after the overload protection of the machine. If the overload protection occurs 3 times within half an hour, the overload fault will not be cleared|
|remoteOnOffEnable|Remote control|0:ON <br> 1:OFF||NO|Number|The machine shuts down and enters standby mode|
|remoteOutputOnOffControl|AC Output ON/OFF|0:ON <br> 1:OFF||NO|Number||
|zeroExportFunction|Zero Export Mode|1:To load <br> 2:To CT||NO|Number|"The feed power is limited according to the setting of ""grid connection power limit"". <br> 1) To load: <br> The inverter can supply power to the backup load and sell power to the grid but the selling power will not exceed the setting value of ""grid connection power limit"" <br> 2) To CT: <br> The inverter can supply power to the backup load, household load and sell power to the grid, but the selling power will not exceed the setting value of ""grid connection power limit"".In this mode, a CT is needed."|
|zeroExportAdjustmentPower|Zero Export Power|-500~500|W|NO|Number|When the grid-connected power is set to 0, it is used to calibrate the sampling deviation to ensure that the inverter will not feed power to the grid|
|clearEnergyStorageLog|Clear Energy Storage Log|1:Clear||NO|Number||
|clearEventLog|Clear Event Log|1:Clear||NO|Number||
|factoryReset|Factory Reset|1:Reset||NO|Number||
|eventLogFunctionEnable|Event Log Function|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|faultLogFunctionEnable|Fault Log Function|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|gfciLevel1ProtectionEnable|GFCI One-level Protection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|gfciLevel2ProtectionEnable|GFCI Two-level Protection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|gfciLevel3ProtectionEnable|GFCI Three-level Protection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|isoDetectionEnable|ISO Detection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|antiIslandingDetectionEnable|Anti-Islanding Detection|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number||
|pvParallelSetting|PV Parallel Set|0:Stand Alone <br> 1:Parallel||NO|Number||
|fixQPencent|FixQ Pencent|-60~60|%|NO|Number||
|bmsHeartbeat|BMS Heartbeat|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge||NO|Number|After enabling, if the battery type is lithium battery and BMS communication fails, it will report BMS communication failure|
|operatedMode|Operated Mode|0:General Mode <br> 1:Backup Mode <br> 2:Eco Mode <br> 3:Gen Mode||NO|Number|"Set inverter operation mode: <br> 1) General mode: <br> In this mode, the load is mainly powered by PV and battery. <br> 2) Backup mode: <br> In this mode, PV gives priority to charging the battery to ensure that the battery is always full. <br> 3) ECO mode: <br> In this mode, you can set a specific time to take power from the grid to charge the battery or use the power of the grid to charge the battery, and operate in general mode outside the set time. <br> 4) Generator mode: <br> In this mode, the load is mainly powered by PV and battery."|
|ecoRule1.ruleMode|ECO Mode 1|0:Disable <br> 1:Charge <br> 2:Discharge  <br> 3:AC Couple||NO|Number|"Economic model rules: <br> 1) Disable: No charging and discharging <br> 2) Charge: Charge the battery from the grid <br> 3) Discharge: Selling power from battery to the grid <br> 4)AC Couple:Spontaneous self-use, waste electricity Internet access"|
